# Developer Guide: Replicating Trendlyne & ETNow Screener Fetching

This helper guide details how to query and parse stock screener datasets from **Trendlyne** and **ETNow** in any external codebase. It covers endpoint locations, required headers, query payloads, parsing logic, and best practices.

---

## 1. Trendlyne Screener Integration

Trendlyne provides fundamental, technical, and valuation screeners. Its "All-in-One Screener" is queryable via a broker-facing web-view endpoint.

### A. Endpoint & Parameters
*   **Base URL**: `https://kayal.trendlyne.com/broker-webview/kayal/all-in-one-screener-data-get/`
*   **Method**: `GET`
*   **Query Parameters**:
    *   `screenpk`: The unique identifier string for the target screener (e.g., `'48091'`, `'515760'`).
    *   `perPageCount`: Page size limit (e.g., `25` or `200`).
    *   `pageNumber`: `0`-indexed page number.
    *   `groupType`: `'all'` (Default).
    *   `groupName`: `''` (Default).

### B. Headers Strategy
To prevent requests from being blocked, pass standard browser-like user agents and a matching Referer header:
```http
User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36 (KHTML, like Gecko) Chrome/120.0.0.0 Safari/537.36
Accept: application/json
Referer: https://kayal.trendlyne.com/
```

### C. JSON Response Structure & Parsing Logic
The API returns a nested layout where the actual stocks are stored as sequential lists mapped by index to headers:
```json
{
  "head": {
    "status": "0",
    "message": "success"
  },
  "body": {
    "screenObj": {
      "title": "Zero Debt Quality Stocks",
      "description": "Companies with zero debt-to-equity ratio and high interest coverage."
    },
    "tableHeaders": [
      {"name": "Stock Name", "parameter": "name"},
      {"name": "LTP", "parameter": "lastPrice"},
      {"name": "PE", "parameter": "pe_ttm"}
    ],
    "tableData": [
      [
        {"value": "19814", "parameter": "stockId"},
        {"value": "Wipro Ltd.", "parameter": "name"},
        {"value": "485.60", "parameter": "lastPrice"},
        {"value": "22.4", "parameter": "pe_ttm"}
      ]
    ]
  }
}
```

#### Parsing Algorithm (Python Example):
```python
def parse_trendlyne_response(json_data):
    if json_data.get("head", {}).get("status") != "0" or "body" not in json_data:
        return []
    
    table_data = json_data["body"].get("tableData", [])
    stocks = []
    
    for row in table_data:
        stock_entry = {}
        for cell in row:
            param_name = cell.get("parameter")
            value = cell.get("value")
            
            # Map standard fields
            if param_name == "stockId":
                stock_entry["stock_id"] = value
            elif param_name == "name":
                stock_entry["name"] = value
            elif param_name == "lastPrice":
                stock_entry["ltp"] = float(value) if value else 0.0
            elif param_name == "changePercent":
                stock_entry["change_percent"] = float(value) if value else 0.0
            else:
                stock_entry[param_name] = value
                
        stocks.append(stock_entry)
        
    return stocks
```

---

## 2. ETNow Screener Integration

Economic Times (ETNow) uses an internal screener database with static query rules (such as filter parameters) associated with unique IDs.

### A. Endpoint & Parameters
*   **Base URL**: `https://screener.indiatimes.com/screener/v2/screenerByScreenerIdForWeb`
*   **Method**: `POST`
*   **Request Headers**:
    ```http
    Content-Type: application/json
    Referer: https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/
    Origin: https://economictimes.indiatimes.com
    User-Agent: Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36
    ```

### B. Payload Structure
The POST body matches this JSON model:
```json
{
  "viewId": 6916,
  "sort": [],
  "pagesize": 20,
  "pageno": 1,
  "deviceId": "web",
  "filterType": "index",
  "filterValue": [],
  "screenerId": "73",
  "queryCondition": "DEBTEQTY <= 0.1 AND NETPROFIT_GROWTH_3YR >= 15"
}
```

*   `screenerId`: The unique ETNow screener ID (e.g. `'73'` for Cash Cows, `'79'` for Zero Debt).
*   `queryCondition`: The SQL-like filtering string that controls query criteria. If you have `et_screeners.json`, load it into your database to map screener IDs to their matching `queryCondition`.

### C. JSON Response Structure & Parsing Logic
The response returns direct flat records inside a unified search data structure:
```json
{
  "searchResult": {
    "searchData": {
      "records": [
        {
          "symbol": "INFY",
          "companyName": "Infosys Ltd.",
          "currentPrice": 1420.50,
          "priceChange": 15.20,
          "percentChange": 1.08,
          "marketCap": 589452.0
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

#### Parsing Algorithm (Node.js/JavaScript Example):
```javascript
async function fetchEtScreener(screenerId, queryCondition) {
  const url = "https://screener.indiatimes.com/screener/v2/screenerByScreenerIdForWeb";
  
  const payload = {
    viewId: 6916,
    sort: [],
    pagesize: 250, // Increase for bulk extractions
    pageno: 1,
    deviceId: "web",
    filterType: "index",
    filterValue: [],
    screenerId: screenerId,
    queryCondition: queryCondition
  };

  const response = await fetch(url, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/json",
      "Referer": "https://economictimes.indiatimes.com/",
      "Origin": "https://economictimes.indiatimes.com",
      "User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (Windows NT 10.0; Win64; x64) AppleWebKit/537.36"
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(payload)
  });

  const json = await response.json();
  const records = json?.searchResult?.searchData?.records || [];
  
  return records.map(r => ({
    symbol: r.symbol || '',
    companyName: r.companyName || r.name || '',
    ltp: parseFloat(r.currentPrice || r.ltp || 0),
    change: parseFloat(r.priceChange || 0),
    changePercent: parseFloat(r.percentChange || 0),
    marketCap: parseFloat(r.marketCap || 0)
  }));
}
```

---

## 3. General Best Practices & Caching

To prevent target hosts from issuing 403 Forbidden blocks or rate-limits:
1.  **Polite Caching**: Cache screener data in a local Redis database or SQLite file for at least 5 minutes (`INTRADAY_CACHE_TTL_MS = 180000`).
2.  **Jittered Requests**: Add a delay of `500ms + random_jitter(100ms)` between calls when running sync loops across multiple screener IDs.
3.  **Symbol Alignment**: Use an alignment table (e.g. `tlid_mapping.csv` or autocomplete lookup) because Trendlyne (`stockId` like `19814`) and ETNow (`symbol` like `INFY`) do not share identical identifier keys.

| Close Within 52 Week Low Zone Close Within 52 Week Low Zone refers to stocks that have recently closed their trading session at a price level that is within the range of their lowest price observed in the past 52 weeks. It suggests that the stock is currently trading near its lowest point in the past year. Utilize this data page to identify stocks that are trading within their 52-week low zone. This information can be useful for investors looking for potential buying opportunities or stocks that may be oversold. However, it is important to conduct further analysis and consider other factors before making investment decisions.
